Title: Духовный регламент, тщанием и повелением всепресветлейшего, державнейшего государя Петра Первого, императора и самодержца всероссийского, по соизволению и приговору всероссийского духовного чина и правительствующего сената, в царствующем Санктпетербурге, в лето от Рождества Христова 1721, сочиненный (The Spiritual Regulation, composed in St Petersburg in 1721)
Creator: The Spiritual Regulation of 1721 (drafted by Feofan Prokopovich), with the Supplement (Pribavlenie) of 1722
Publisher: Москва: в Синодальной типографии (Moscow: Synodal Press)
Publication Date: 1856
Rights: Public Domain (published 1856, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: rus
Source: Internet Archive item duhovnyjreglamenttschaniemipoveleniemvs66 (https://archive.org/details/duhovnyjreglamenttschaniemipoveleniemvs66), the item's own duhovnyjreglamenttschaniemipoveleniemvs66_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1856. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: c5f68e53adb9ed390cf9f6becb42a70b8699eabc1c795f15ad48184dd0e6bfd9.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Russian online-library scan (Russian State Library stamp); usable OCR in the pre-reform orthography, with the title-page leaves garbled and the OCR misreading the 1721 of the title as 1791; the title page reads Moscow 1856.
Content note: The Regulation in its original Russian in the Synod's own reprint: Peter's manifesto, the three parts on the reasons for the Spiritual College, the matters it governs and its officers, the oath of its members, and the Supplement on the clergy and monastics, including the articles on confession that require a confessor to report a penitent's declared intent of treason.
